# MATH  
  
#### Overview

**Description:**

Provides math functions, such as trigonometric, exponential, logarithmic functions,and perform operations on complex numbers. 

**Since:**

1.0

**Version:**

1.0

#### Summary

#### [h2]Files

File Name | Description  
---|---  
[complex.h](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/complex_8h-0000001055108019) | Declares functions to perform operations on complex numbers.   
[fenv.h](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/fenv_8h-0000001055189443) | Declares specific functions for performing operations for floating-point exceptions and rounding modes.   
[math.h](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math_8h-0000001055547980) | Provides common math functions.   
  
#### [h2]Data Structures

Data Structure Name | Description  
---|---  
[fenv_t](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/fenv__t-0000001054918199) | Defines the floating-point environment.   
  
#### [h2]Macros

Macro Name and Value | Description  
---|---  
__complex _Complex | Indicates the type of a complex number.   
__FE_INVALID 1 | Indicates an invalid floating-point exception. At least one parameter value is not defined in the function.   
__FE_DIVBYZERO 2 | Indicates a pole error exception. The return value is asymptotically infinite when the divisor is zero or the input is asymptotic to zero.   
__FE_OVERFLOW 4 | Indicates an overflow floating-point exception. The result is too large to be represented in the normal return value.   
__FE_UNDERFLOW 8 | Indicates an underflow floating-point exception. The result is too small to be represented in the normal return value.   
__FE_INEXACT 16 | Indicates an inexact floating-point exception.   
__FE_ALL_EXCEPT 31 | Indicates bitwise OR of all the preceding supported floating-point exceptions.   
__FE_TONEAREST 0 | Indicates rounding towards the nearest integer.   
__FE_DOWNWARD 0x800000 | Indicates rounding towards negative infinity.   
__FE_UPWARD 0x400000 | Indicates rounding towards positive infinity.   
__FE_TOWARDZERO 0xc00000 | Indicates rounding towards zero.   
__FE_DFL_ENV ((const[fenv_t](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/fenv__t-0000001054918199) *) -1) | Defines the default floating-point environment.   
__NAN (0.0f/0.0f) | Indicates an undefined or unrepresentable value.   
__INFINITY 1e5000f | Indicates an infinity.   
__HUGE_VALF[INFINITY](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga956e2723d559858d08644ac99146e910) | Indicates a float infinity.   
__HUGE_VAL ((double)[INFINITY](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga956e2723d559858d08644ac99146e910)) | Indicates a double infinity.   
__HUGE_VALL ((long double)[INFINITY](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga956e2723d559858d08644ac99146e910)) | Indicates a long double infinity.   
__MATH_ERRNO 1 | Indicates an error that occurs in floating-point operations.   
__MATH_ERREXCEPT 2 | Indicates a floating-point exception.   
__math_errhandling 2 | Indicates the error handling mechanism employed by math functions.   
__FP_ILOGBNAN (-1-0x7fffffff) | Indicates the calculation result of [ilogb](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga600dd249210d15af31b9a29c2d09d255)([NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)).   
__FP_ILOGB0[FP_ILOGBNAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga01e2e5be97c739a5bdcbc619b44f9742) | Indicates the calculation result of [ilogb](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga600dd249210d15af31b9a29c2d09d255)(0).   
__FP_NAN 0 | Indicates a Not-a-Number (NaN) value.   
__FP_INFINITE 1 | Indicates an infinity.   
__FP_ZERO 2 | Indicates value **0**.   
__FP_SUBNORMAL 3 | Indicates a subnormal.   
__FP_NORMAL 4 | Indicates a normal value.   
[isinf](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga2a266c767456556e5c0da234b602b18b)(x) | Checks whether the floating-point value **x** is an infinity.

((void)(y),1) : [isnan](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga2e1baae9134e580910322362dc23290e)((y))) | Checks whether floating-point value **x** or **y** is unordered.

#### Details

#### Macro Definition

#### [h2]isfinite
    
    #define isfinite( x)
    
    **Values:**
    
    ( \
    
    
    
    
    sizeof(x) == sizeof(float) ? (__FLOAT_BITS(x) & 0x7fffffff) < 0x7f800000 : \
    
    
    
    
    sizeof(x) == sizeof(double) ? (__DOUBLE_BITS(x) & -1ULL>>1) < 0x7ffULL<<52 : \
    
    
    
    
    __fpclassifyl(x) > [FP_INFINITE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga4f64bcc64f8b84dd9daf5a4e56abf0ca))
    
    
    

**Description:**

Checks whether the floating-point value **x** is finite. 

A finite value is any value that is neither an infinity nor a NaN value. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is finite; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]isgreater
    
    #define isgreater( x,  y )   __tg_pred_2(x, y, __isgreater)

**Description:**

Checks whether floating-point value **x** is greater than **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
y | Indicates the floating-point value **y** to check.   
  
**Attention:**

If either or both of **x** and **y** are a NaN value, an exception occurs. This function requires hardware support. 

**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is greater than **y** ; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]isgreaterequal
    
    #define isgreaterequal( x,  y )   __tg_pred_2(x, y, __isgreaterequal)

**Description:**

Checks whether floating-point value **x** is greater than or equal to **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
y | Indicates the floating-point value **y** to check.   
  
**Attention:**

If either or both of **x** and **y** are a NaN value, an exception occurs. This function requires hardware support. 

**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is greater than or equal to **y** ; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]isinf
    
    #define isinf( x)
    
    **Values:**
    
    ( \
    
    
    
    
    sizeof(x) == sizeof(float) ? (__FLOAT_BITS(x) & 0x7fffffff) == 0x7f800000 : \
    
    
    
    
    sizeof(x) == sizeof(double) ? (__DOUBLE_BITS(x) & -1ULL>>1) == 0x7ffULL<<52 : \
    
    
    
    
    __fpclassifyl(x) == [FP_INFINITE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga4f64bcc64f8b84dd9daf5a4e56abf0ca))
    
    
    

**Description:**

Checks whether the floating-point value **x** is an infinity. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is an infinity; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]isless
    
    #define isless( x,  y )   __tg_pred_2(x, y, __isless)

**Description:**

Checks whether floating-point value **x** is less than **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
y | Indicates the floating-point value **y** to check.   
  
**Attention:**

If either or both of **x** and **y** are a NaN value, an exception occurs. This function requires hardware support. 

**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is less than **y** ; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]islessequal
    
    #define islessequal( x,  y )   __tg_pred_2(x, y, __islessequal)

**Description:**

Checks whether floating-point value **x** is less than or equal to **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
y | Indicates the floating-point value **y** to check.   
  
**Attention:**

If either or both of **x** and **y** are a NaN value, an exception occurs. This function requires hardware support. 

**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is less than or equal to **y** ; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]islessgreater
    
    #define islessgreater( x,  y )   __tg_pred_2(x, y, __islessgreater)

**Description:**

Checks whether floating-point value **x** is either less than or greater than **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the floating-point value **x** to check.   
y | Indicates the floating-point value **y** to check.   
  
**Attention:**

If either or both of **x** and **y** are a NaN value, an exception occurs. This function requires hardware support. 

**Returns:**

Returns **1** if **x** is either less than or greater than **y** ; returns **0** otherwise. 

#### [h2]isnan
    
    #define isnan( x)
    
    **Values:**
    
    ( \
    
    
    
    
    sizeof(x) == sizeof(float) ? (__FLOAT_BITS(x) & 0x7fffffff) > 0x7f800000 : \
    
    
    
    
    sizeof(x) == sizeof(double) ? (__DOUBLE_BITS(x) & -1ULL>>1) > 0x7ffULL<<52 : \
    
    
    
    
    __fpclassifyl(x) == [FP_NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga1d481cc6e6a8ff729147ed46e61a4c9f))

#### [h2]isnormal

    sizeof(x) == sizeof(double) ? ((__DOUBLE_BITS(x)+(1ULL<<52)) & -1ULL>>1) >= 1ULL<<53 : \
    
    
    
    
    __fpclassifyl(x) == [FP_NORMAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab9e29bb9e853934c4ba647c840b7e5de))

#### Function

#### [h2]acos()
    
    double acos (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc cosine of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value whose arc cosine is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc cosine of **x** in radians, ranging from 0 to [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3). If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+1** , this function returns **+0**. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns a NaN value and reports a domain error. If **x** is out of the range [-1, 1], this function returns a NaN value, reports a domain error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception.

#### [h2]atan()
    
    double atan (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value whose arc tangent is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the principal value of the arc tangent of **x** in radians, ranging from -[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) to [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f). If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f). 

#### [h2]atan2()
    
    double atan2 (double y, double x )

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of two double values **x** and **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
y | Indicates the double value **y** for calculating the arc tangent.   
x | Indicates the double value **x** for calculating the arc tangent.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the principal value of the arc tangent of y/x in radians, ranging from **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** to **[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is less than **0** , this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is greater than **0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is less than **0** and **x** is either **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **-[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**. If **y** is greater than **0** and **x** is either **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**. If **x** or **y** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is **-0** , this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is **+0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is a finite value greater than or less than **0** , and **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is a finite value greater than or less than **0** , and **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a finite value, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)** or **-[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**, respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)** or **-3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)**, respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)** or **-[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)**, respectively. 

#### [h2]atan2f()

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of two float values **x** and **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
y | Indicates the float value **y** for calculating the arc tangent.

**Returns:**

Returns the principal value of the arc tangent of y/x in radians, ranging from **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** to **[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is less than **0** , this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is greater than **0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is less than **0** and **x** is either **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **-[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**. If **y** is greater than **0** and **x** is either **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**. If **x** or **y** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is **-0** , this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is **+0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is a finite value greater than or less than **0** , and **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is a finite value greater than or less than **0** , and **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a finite value, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)** or **-[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**, respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)** or **-3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)**, respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)** or **-[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)**, respectively. 

#### [h2]atan2l()
    
    long double atan2l (long double y, long double x )

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of two long double values **x** and **y**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
y | Indicates the long double value **y** for calculating the arc tangent.   
x | Indicates the long double value **x** for calculating the arc tangent.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the principal value of the arc tangent of y/x in radians, ranging from **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** to **[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is less than **0** , this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is greater than **0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is less than **0** and **x** is either **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **-[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**. If **y** is greater than **0** and **x** is either **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**. If **x** or **y** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is **-0** , this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is **+0** or **-0** , and **x** is **+0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is a finite value greater than or less than **0** , and **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)** or **-[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)**, respectively. If **y** is a finite value greater than or less than **0** , and **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a finite value, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)** or **-[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)**, respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)** or **-3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)**, respectively. If **y** is a positive or negative infinity, and **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)** or **-[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)**, respectively. 

#### [h2]atanf()
    
    float atanf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value whose arc tangent is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the principal value of the arc tangent of **x** in radians, ranging from -[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) to [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f). If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) or -[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f), respectively. 

#### [h2]atanh()
    
    double atanh (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the inverse hyperbolic tangent of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value whose inverse hyperbolic tangent is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the inverse hyperbolic tangent of **x**. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is **+1** or **-1** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971), reports a pole error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If the absolute value of **x** is greater than **1** , this function returns a NaN value, reports a domain error, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception. 

#### [h2]atanhf()
    
    float atanhf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the inverse hyperbolic tangent of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value whose inverse hyperbolic tangent is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the inverse hyperbolic tangent of **x**. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is **+1** or **-1** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971), reports a pole error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If the absolute value of **x** is greater than **1** , this function returns a NaN value, reports a domain error, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception.

Calculates the inverse hyperbolic tangent of the long double value **x**.

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value whose inverse hyperbolic tangent is to be calculated.

Returns the inverse hyperbolic tangent of **x**. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is **+1** or **-1** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971), reports a pole error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. #### [h2]atanl()
    
    long double atanl (long double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of the long double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value whose arc tangent is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the principal value of the arc tangent of **x** in radians, ranging from -[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) to [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f). If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) or -[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f), respectively. 

#### [h2]cabs()
    
    double cabs (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the absolute value of the complex number **z**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose absolute value is to be obtained.   
  
**Attention:**

If an error occurs or **z** is a positive or negative infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), the returned value is calculated based on the formula [hypot](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6d6a905f61d535f8454190433b8c0ea5)([cimag](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6c3d7c875ae93700b0a2a32db24b37d3)(z) + [creal](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga24b1a511efdb973354d81646c5c3bb81)(z)). 

**Returns:**

Returns the absolute value of **z** if the operation is successful. 

#### [h2]cabsf()
    
    float cabsf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the absolute value of the complex number **z**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose absolute value is to be obtained.   
  
**Attention:**

If an error occurs or **z** is a positive or negative infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), the returned value is calculated based on the formula [hypot](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6d6a905f61d535f8454190433b8c0ea5)([cimag](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6c3d7c875ae93700b0a2a32db24b37d3)(z) + [creal](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga24b1a511efdb973354d81646c5c3bb81)(z)). 

**Returns:**

Returns the absolute value of **z** if the operation is successful. 

#### [h2]cabsl()
    
    long double cabsl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the absolute value of the complex number **z**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose absolute value is to be obtained.   
  
**Attention:**

If an error occurs or **z** is a positive or negative infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), the returned value is calculated based on the formula [hypot](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6d6a905f61d535f8454190433b8c0ea5)([cimag](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6c3d7c875ae93700b0a2a32db24b37d3)(z) + [creal](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga24b1a511efdb973354d81646c5c3bb81)(z)).

#### [h2]cacos()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cacos (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose arc cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is **0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \- 0i**. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is an infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \- [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3) \- [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22) \- [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22) \- [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \- [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cacosf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cacosf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose arc cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is **0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \- 0i**. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is an infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)-[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3) \- [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22) \- [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22) \- [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i**. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \- [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cacosh()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cacosh (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the inverse hyperbolic cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose inverse hyperbolic cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the inverse hyperbolic cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **0** and **y** is **0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ 3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ 3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cacoshl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cacoshl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the inverse hyperbolic cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose inverse hyperbolic cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the inverse hyperbolic cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **0** and **y** is **0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ 3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cacosl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cacosl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose arc cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is **0** , this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \- 0i**. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is an infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)-[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3) \- [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22) \- [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22) \- [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)i**. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \- [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]carg()
    
    double carg (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the phase angle of the complex number **z**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose phase angle is to be obtained.   
  
**Attention:**

If an error occurs or **z** is a positive or negative infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), the returned value is calculated based on the formula [atan2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga349377202ca14f79eb335b1a421fe4d8)([cimag](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6c3d7c875ae93700b0a2a32db24b37d3)(z) + [creal](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga24b1a511efdb973354d81646c5c3bb81)(z)). 

**Returns:**

Returns the phase angle of **z** if the operation is successful.

Calculates the phase angle of the complex number **z**.

**Returns:**

Returns the phase angle of **z** if the operation is successful.

    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) casin (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc sine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose arc sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain. #### [h2]casinf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) casinf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc sine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose arc sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.
