If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]casinhl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) casinhl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the inverse hyperbolic sine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose inverse hyperbolic sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the inverse hyperbolic sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

#### [h2]catan()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) catan (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the arc tangent of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose arc tangent is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the arc tangent of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is **1** and **y** is **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)** and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cbrt()
    
    double cbrt (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the cube root of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value whose cube root is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cube root if the operation is successful; returns the passed **x** if **x** is **+0** , **-0** , a positive or negative infinity, or a NaN value. 

#### [h2]cbrtf()
    
    float cbrtf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the cube root of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value whose cube root is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cube root if the operation is successful; returns the passed **x** if **x** is **+0** , **-0** , a positive or negative infinity, or a NaN value. 

#### [h2]cbrtl()
    
    long double cbrtl (long double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the cube root of the long double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value whose cube root is to be calculated.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cube root if the operation is successful; returns the passed **x** if **x** is **+0** , **-0** , a positive or negative infinity, or a NaN value. 

#### [h2]ccos()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ccos (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971). If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]ccosf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ccosf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359). If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]ccosh()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ccosh (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the hyperbolic cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose hyperbolic cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]ccoshf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ccoshf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the hyperbolic cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose hyperbolic cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

Calculates the hyperbolic cosine of the complex number **z**.

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose hyperbolic cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182). #### [h2]ccosl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ccosl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the cosine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose cosine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cosine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182). If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]ceil()
    
    double ceil (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the minimum integer greater than or equal to the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value **x** to compare with.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the minimum integer greater than or equal to **x** if the operation is successful; returns the passed **x** if **x** is an integer, **+0** , **-0** , a NaN value, or an infinity. 

#### [h2]ceilf()
    
    float ceilf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the minimum integer greater than or equal to the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value **x** to compare with.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the minimum integer greater than or equal to **x** if the operation is successful; returns the passed **x** if **x** is an integer, **+0** , **-0** , a NaN value, or an infinity. 

#### [h2]ceill()
    
    long double ceill (long double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the minimum integer greater than or equal to the long double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value **x** to compare with.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the minimum integer greater than or equal to **x** if the operation is successful; returns the passed **x** if **x** is an integer, **+0** , **-0** , a NaN value, or an infinity. 

#### [h2]cexp()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cexp (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the base-e exponential of the complex number **z**. 

The mathematical formula is e^z. z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose base-e exponential is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the base-e exponential of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971). If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is an infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **0**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cexpf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cexpf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the base-e exponential of the complex number **z**. 

The mathematical formula is e^z. z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose base-e exponential is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the base-e exponential of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359). If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is an infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **0**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]cexpl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cexpl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

Calculates the base-e exponential of the complex number **z**.

If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is an infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **0**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is neither an infinity nor **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

#### [h2]clog()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) clog (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the natural (base-e) logarithm of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose natural (base-e) logarithm is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the natural (base-e) logarithm of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **-0** and **y** is **+0** , this function returns **-[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i** and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception. If **x** and **y** are both **+0** , this function returns **-[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ 0i** and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ 3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ 0i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]clogf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) clogf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the natural (base-e) logarithm of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1)

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose natural (base-e) logarithm is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the natural (base-e) logarithm of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **-0** and **y** is **+0** , this function returns **-[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i** and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception. If **x** and **y** are both **+0** , this function returns **-[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ 0i** and throws the [FE_DIVBYZERO](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab1317930189d1a14841893fb4bd9b5a0) exception. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ 3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ 0i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.
