If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI_2](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga958e4508ed28ee5cc04249144312c15f)i**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gae71449b1cc6e6250b91f539153a7a0d3)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ 3*[M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ 0i**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [M_PI_4](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaeb24420b096a677f3a2dc5a72b36bf22)i**. If **x** is an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not an infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]conj()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) conj (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the conjugate of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose conjugate is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the conjugate of **z** , which is equal to x - yi. 

#### [h2]conjf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) conjf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the conjugate of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose conjugate is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the conjugate of **z** , which is equal to x - yi. 

#### [h2]conjl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) conjl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the conjugate of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose conjugate is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the conjugate of **z** , which is equal to x - yi. 

#### [h2]copysign()
    
    double copysign (double x, double y )

**Description:**

Generates a value by combining the magnitude of the double value **x** and the sign of the double value **y**. 

For example, if **x** is **42.0** and **y** is **-1.0** , value **-42.0** will be generated. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value **x** whose magnitude will be used.   
y | Indicates the double value **y** whose sign will be used.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the generated value if the operation is successful; returns a NaN value with the sign of **y** if **x** is a NaN value. 

#### [h2]copysignf()
    
    float copysignf (float x, float y )

**Description:**

Generates a value by combining the magnitude of the float value **x** and the sign of the float value **y**. 

For example, if **x** is **42.0** and **y** is **-1.0** , value **-42.0** will be generated. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value **x** whose magnitude will be used.   
y | Indicates the float value **y** whose sign will be used.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the generated value if the operation is successful; returns a NaN value with the sign of **y** if **x** is a NaN value. 

#### [h2]copysignl()
    
    long double copysignl (long double x, long double y )

**Description:**

Generates a value by combining the magnitude of the long double value **x** and the sign of the long double value **y**. 

For example, if **x** is **42.0** and **y** is **-1.0** , value **-42.0** will be generated. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value **x** whose magnitude will be used.   
y | Indicates the long double value **y** whose magnitude will be used.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the generated value if the operation is successful; returns a NaN value with the sign of **y** if **x** is a NaN value. 

#### [h2]cos()
    
    double cos (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the cosine of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cosine of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns a NaN value, reports a domain error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. 

#### [h2]cosf()
    
    float cosf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the cosine of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cosine of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns a NaN value, reports a domain error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. 

#### [h2]cosh()
    
    double cosh (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the hyperbolic cosine of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic cosine of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971), sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception. 

#### [h2]coshf()
    
    float coshf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the hyperbolic cosine of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic cosine of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359), sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception.

    long double coshl (long double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the hyperbolic cosine of the long double value **x**.

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value.

Returns the hyperbolic cosine of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **1**. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182), sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception.

Calculates the cosine of the long double value **x**.

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the cosine of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns a NaN value, reports a domain error, sets **errno** to [EDOM](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga5fe247e079b591a68e0fdbf7caec5b70), and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. 

#### [h2]cpow()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cpow (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) x, double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z )

**Description:**

Calculates the value of **x** raised to the **z** power. 

The mathematical formula is x^z. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the base (a double complex number).   
z | Indicates the exponent (a double complex number).   
  
**Attention:**

If an error occurs or **x** or **z** is a positive or negative infinity or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), the returned value is calculated based on the formula [cexp](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab9c654c52efefb54d06d52aa1a1c1827)(z*[clog](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaa39c3557d8db10c19571bc0b63fd5529)(x)). 

**Returns:**

Returns the value of **x** raised to the **z** power if the operation is successful.

    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cpowf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) x, float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z )

Name | Description  
---|---

#### [h2]cproj()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cproj (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the projection of the complex number **z** onto the Riemann sphere. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose projection onto the Riemann sphere is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the projection of **z** onto the Riemann sphere if the operation is successful. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is an infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971). If **x** is an infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ i*[copysign](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad8d1296592a3e783acdd0de21ff58e2e)(0.0, [cimag](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6c3d7c875ae93700b0a2a32db24b37d3)(z)). 

#### [h2]cprojf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) cprojf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the projection of the complex number **z** onto the Riemann sphere. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose projection onto the Riemann sphere is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the projection of **z** onto the Riemann sphere if the operation is successful. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is an infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359). If **x** is an infinity, this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ i*[copysign](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad8d1296592a3e783acdd0de21ff58e2e)(0.0, [cimag](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga6c3d7c875ae93700b0a2a32db24b37d3)(z)).

#### [h2]csin()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) csin (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the sine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

Calculates the sine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]csinh()

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose hyperbolic sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. #### [h2]csinhf()
    
    float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) csinhf (float [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the hyperbolic sine of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a float value) whose hyperbolic sine is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the hyperbolic sine of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is **0** and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is **0** and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive finite value and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is **0** , this function returns [HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)*([cos](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga5e7b53a694b3cf0f2d7debd444140fbd)(y) + i[sin](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaad4af25633b071b0e2cdb8ebd87eca77)(y))**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain) and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **+(-)[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** (the sign bit of the real part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]csqrt()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) csqrt (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the square root of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose square root is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the square root of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** and **y** is **+0** , this function returns **+0 + 0i**. If **x** is neither an infinity nor [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** is a finite value or [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ 0i**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is any value, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i**. If **x** is a negative infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) +(-) [HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)i** (the sign bit of the imaginary part of the returned value is uncertain). If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359) \+ 0i**. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]ctan()
    
    double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ctan (double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the tangent of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a double value) whose tangent is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the tangent of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **1**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **1**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**.

If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **1**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **1**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]ctanl()
    
    long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) ctanl (long double [complex](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga0fa4878c968311979d497ccc322e0b9b) z)

**Description:**

Calculates the tangent of the complex number **z**. 

z = x + yi, where i = [sqrt](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab87521a75ef827a85807f89a6c10629a)(-1) 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
z | Indicates the complex number (a long double value) whose tangent is to be obtained.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the tangent of **z** if the operation is successful. If **x** and **y** are both **0** , this function returns **0**. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is a positive infinity, this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is not an infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and may throw the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is a positive finite value, this function returns **1**. If **x** and **y** are both positive infinity, this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive infinity and **y** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **1**. If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is **0** , this function returns [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc). If **x** is [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) and **y** is not **0** , this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i** and throws the [FE_INVALID](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga638c8489adb1fac7204b07ece42998ae) exception. If **x** and **y** are both [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc), this function returns **[NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc) \+ [NAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga8abfcc76130f3f991d124dd22d7e69bc)i**. 

#### [h2]erf()
    
    double erf (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the error function of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the error function if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns **+1** or **-1** , respectively. If an underflow range error occurs, this function returns **2*x/sqrt(pi)** and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception. 

#### [h2]erfc()
    
    double erfc (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the complementary error function of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the complementary error function if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+0**. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+2**. If an underflow range error occurs and an unrepresentable value is generated, this function returns **0.0**. If an underflow range error occurs and a representable value is generated, this function returns **0** and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception.

    float erfcf (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the complementary error function of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the complementary error function if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+0**. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+2**. If an underflow range error occurs and an unrepresentable value is generated, this function returns **0.0**. If an underflow range error occurs and a representable value is generated, this function returns **0** and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception.

x | Indicates the long double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the complementary error function if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **1**. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns **+0**. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+2**. If an underflow range error occurs and an unrepresentable value is generated, this function returns **0.0**. If an underflow range error occurs and a representable value is generated, this function returns **0** and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception.

    float erff (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates the error function of the float value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the error function if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. Calculates the error function of the long double value **x**.

Name | Description

x | Indicates the long double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the error function if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive or negative infinity, this function returns **+1** or **-1** , respectively. If an underflow range error occurs, this function returns **2*x/sqrt(pi)** and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception. 

#### [h2]exp()
    
    double exp (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the base-e exponential function of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the base-e exponential function of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+0**. If an underflow range error occurs, this function returns **0** , sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns **+[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)**, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception.

**Description:**

Calculates the base-10 exponential function of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value **x** used as the exponent.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the base-10 exponential function of **x** if the operation is successful.

Returns the base-10 exponential function of **x** if the operation is successful.

Calculates the base-10 exponential function of the long double value **x**.

Name | Description

#### [h2]exp2()
    
    double exp2 (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates the base-2 exponential function of the double value **x**. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the base-2 exponential function of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **+0**. If an underflow range error occurs, this function returns **0** , sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_UNDERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga57b80dfe7d5ce60c3c76e517fce89ffe) exception. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns **+[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)**, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception.

#### [h2]expm1()
    
    double expm1 (double x)

**Description:**

Calculates e raised to the power of the double value **x** minus one, that is, (e^x)-1. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns e raised to the power of **x** minus one if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **-1**. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns **+[HUGE_VAL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gaf2164b2db92d8a0ed3838ad5c28db971)**, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception. 

#### [h2]expm1f()
    
    float expm1f (float x)

**Description:**

Calculates e raised to the power of the float value **x** minus one. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns e raised to the power of **x** minus one if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **-1**. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns **+[HUGE_VALF](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gacd69981d54c27fe0ff514645dbfc6359)**, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception. 

#### [h2]expm1l()
    
    long double expm1l (long double x)

**Description:**

Calculates e raised to the power of the long double value **x** minus one. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns e raised to the power of **x** minus one if the operation is successful. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns a NaN value. If **x** is **+0** or **-0** , this function returns **+0** or **-0** , respectively. If **x** is a positive infinity, this function returns a positive infinity. If **x** is a negative infinity, this function returns **-1**. If an overflow range error occurs, this function returns **+[HUGE_VALL](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gab8b359c356d4311bf5d4ae6c03f43182)**, sets **errno** to [ERANGE](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__gaa1591a4f3a86360108de5b9ba34980ca), and throws the [FE_OVERFLOW](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__gad373306add36e7227d9c9620b6962323) exception.

#### [h2]frexp()
    
    double frexp (double x, int * exp )

**Description:**

Decomposes the double value **x** into a significand and an integral exponent for 2. 

The absolute value of the significand ranges from 0.5 (included) to 1 (excluded). x = significand * 2^exponent 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value **x** to decompose.   
exp | Indicates the pointer to the exponent.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the binary significand of **x** if the operation is successful; returns **0** if **x** is **0** (**exp** is also **0**); returns a NaN value if **x** is a NaN value (**exp** is not specified); returns a positive or negative infinity if **x** is a positive or negative infinity, respectively (**exp** is not specified). 

#### [h2]frexpf()
    
    float frexpf (float x, int * exp )

**Description:**

Decomposes float value **x** into a binary significand and an integral exponent for 2. 

The absolute value of the significand ranges from 0.5 (included) to 1 (excluded). x = significand * 2^exponent 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value to decompose.   
exp | Indicates the pointer to an integer where the exponent is stored.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the binary significand of **x** if the operation is successful; returns **0** if **x** is **0** (**exp** is also **0**); returns a NaN value if **x** is a NaN value (**exp** is not specified); returns a positive or negative infinity if **x** is a positive or negative infinity (**exp** is not specified). 

#### [h2]frexpl()
    
    long double frexpl (long double x, int * exp )

**Description:**

Decomposes long double value **x** into a binary significand and an integral exponent for 2. 

The absolute value of the significand ranges from 0.5 (included) to 1 (excluded). x = significand * 2^exponent 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value to decompose.   
exp | Indicates the pointer to an integer where the exponent is stored.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the binary significand of **x** if the operation is successful; returns **0** if **x** is **0** (**exp** is also **0**); returns a NaN value if **x** is a NaN value (**exp** is not specified); returns a positive or negative infinity if **x** is a positive or negative infinity (**exp** is not specified).

#### [h2]ilogb()
    
    int ilogb (double x)

**Description:**

Obtains the integral part of the logarithm of double value **x**. 

**FLT_RADIX** is used as the base for the logarithm. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the double value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the integral part of the logarithm of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **0** , this function returns [FP_ILOGB0](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga9ec0e31304d15c42262b2c4a3b58443c) and reports a domain error. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns [FP_ILOGBNAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga01e2e5be97c739a5bdcbc619b44f9742) and reports a domain error. If **x** is positive or negative infinity, this function returns [INT_MAX](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga9ec306f36d50c7375e74f0d1c55a3a67) and reports a domain error. 

#### [h2]ilogbf()
    
    int ilogbf (float x)

**Description:**

Obtains the integral part of the logarithm of float value **x**. 

**FLT_RADIX** is used as the base for the logarithm. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the float value.   
  
**Returns:**

Returns the integral part of the logarithm of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **0** , this function returns [FP_ILOGB0](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga9ec0e31304d15c42262b2c4a3b58443c) and reports a domain error. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns [FP_ILOGBNAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga01e2e5be97c739a5bdcbc619b44f9742) and reports a domain error. If **x** is positive or negative infinity, this function returns [INT_MAX](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga9ec306f36d50c7375e74f0d1c55a3a67) and reports a domain error.

    int ilogbl (long double x)

**Description:**

Obtains the integral part of the logarithm of the long double value **x**. 

**FLT_RADIX** is used as the base for the logarithm. 

**Parameters:**

Name | Description  
---|---  
x | Indicates the long double value.

Returns the integral part of the logarithm of **x** if the operation is successful. If **x** is **0** , this function returns [FP_ILOGB0](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga9ec0e31304d15c42262b2c4a3b58443c) and reports a domain error. If **x** is a NaN value, this function returns [FP_ILOGBNAN](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/math-0000001055228010#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055228010__ga01e2e5be97c739a5bdcbc619b44f9742) and reports a domain error. If **x** is positive or negative infinity, this function returns [INT_MAX](https://device.harmonyos.com/cn/docs/develop/apiref/utils-0000001055308029#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000001055308029__ga9ec306f36d50c7375e74f0d1c55a3a67) and reports a domain error.
